*** ** * ** ***

Each version of an AndroidX library passes through four release channels while
being developed. This page lists the components that have been updated to a new
alpha version. To view the entire release history for a particular component
back thru January 2018, select it in the left-side menu. See the
[Archive](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/releases/archive.md.txt) section for release notes prior
to 2018.

To view the current versions for every component,
see the [versions page](https://developer.android.com/jetpack/androidx/versions.md.txt).
